Non-normal modal and conditional logics.

To prove the ULIP for logics, we need to extend this concept to sequent calculi, similar to the case of the UIP. If a sequent calculus has this form of the ULIP, then its corresponding logic has the ULIP as well. The summary of the results is provided in the following theorem.

Theorem 56

(Positive results,
[
4
,
3
]
)
.

The following have the ULIP (hence the UIP and LIP):

•

Non-normal modal logics:
\mathsf{E}
,
\mathsf{M}
,
\mathsf{EN}
,
\mathsf{MN}
,
\mathsf{MC}
, and
K
.

•

Conditional logics:
\mathsf{CE}
,
\mathsf{CM}
,
\mathsf{CEN}
,
\mathsf{CMN}
,
\mathsf{CMC}
,
\mathsf{CK}
, and
\mathsf{CKID}
.

•

The intuitionistic monotone modal logic
\mathsf{iM}
.

Theorem 57

(Negative results,
[
4
]
)
.

•

The logics
\mathsf{CKCEM}
and
\mathsf{CKCEMID}
do
not
have the ULIP (but they have the UIP). Hence, they cannot have a fully terminating semi-analytic calculus.

•

Non-normal modal logics
\mathsf{EC}
and
\mathsf{ECN}
and conditional logics
\mathsf{CEC}
and
\mathsf{CECN}
do
not
have the CIP (and hence no U(L)IP). Therefore, they cannot have a semi-analytic calculus.

5
Interpolation Using Generalizations of Sequent Calculi

For many logics, no cut-free sequent calculus is known. While certain types of restricted cuts do not impede the Maehara method (see
\cref
sect:role_of_cut), it is often easier to find a cut-free
hypersequent
,
nested sequent
, or
labelled sequent
calculus for a logic than a (semi-)analytic sequent calculus. In addition, the former are bettter suited to demonstrate the LIP. In this section we show how to adapt the Maehara method to these generalizations of sequents.

We aim to provide a general manual on how to construct interpolants to further explore interpolation properties for other logics of the reader’s interest besides the logics treated in this section. We do this by presenting a reverse engineering strategy. What we mean by this is that we will analyze the form of the rules, which will guide us in coming up with the correct constructions of interpolants.

Since hypersequents and nested sequents can usually be translated into the labelled sequent format and there are powerful general methods of constructing cut-free labelled sequent calculi based on Kripke semantics of the logic
[
68
,
67
]
, we first focus on labelled sequents in
\cref
sect:multi,sect:lab_interp for modal logics over
{\sf CPC}
. In
\cref
sec:hyper, we then briefly explain how to adapt this method to hypersequents and nested sequents.

The method described in this section is sometimes criticized for the explicit use of semantic elements. In this respect, we would like to cite the alternative formulation of the method in
[
59
]
that avoids such explicit use.

5.1
Labelled Sequents

A
labelled sequent
(over a language
\mathcal{L}
) is a sequent (over
\mathcal{L}
) enriched with labels: every formula
\varphi
in the sequent is supplied with some label
i
, resulting in a labelled formula
{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}
. Labels
i
can be taken from any set. W.l.o.g., we will use integer labels from
\mathbb{N}
.

The presented method of proving interpolation was originally developed by Fitting and Kuznets for nested sequents
[
25
]
and later adapted to labelled sequents by Kuznets in
[
55
]
. Here we largely follow
[
56
]
, where additional details can be found. As in
\cref
sec:basics, we will present general conditions guaranteeing that interpolation holds, but based on Kripke frame conditions rather than the sequent rule shape.

Like labelled sequents themselves, this method relies on the Kripke semantics. In particular, labels
i
are interpreted as Kripke worlds.
Whereas standard sequents
\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta
consist of two multisets of formulas (antecedent
\Gamma
and succedent
\Delta
), labelled sequents
\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta
are comprised of two multisets
\Gamma
and
\Delta
of
labelled formulas
plus relational part
\mathcal{R}
. This
\mathcal{R}
consists of relational atoms of the form
iRj
, where
i
and
j
are labels and
R
is the Kripke accessibility relation.
1
1

1

Multiple relations and/or
n
-ary relations may be used according to the Kripke semantics.

\mathtt{L}(S)
denotes the set of all labels occurring in
S
. For instance,
\mathtt{L}(1R2,\ {2}\mathord{:}{\varphi},\ {4}\mathord{:}{p}\ \Rightarrow\ {4}\mathord{:}{\psi})=\{1,2,4\}
. We write
i\in S
to mean
i\in\mathtt{L}(S)
.

Definition 58

(Labelled semantics)
.

Let
\mathcal{M}=(W,R,V)
be a Kripke model.
For a labelled sequent
S=\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta
, a function
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\colon X\to W
is called an
interpretation of
S
into
\mathcal{M}
iff
X\supseteq\mathtt{L}(S)
, i.e.,
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
interprets all labels of
S
, and
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}i\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}R\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.2965pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-3.2965pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 3.2965pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.90543pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-2.90543pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 2.90543pt$}}}j\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.2965pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-3.2965pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 3.2965pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.90543pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-2.90543pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 2.90543pt$}}}
in
\mathcal{M}
whenever
iRj\in\mathcal{R}
.
For such an interpretation,
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta
iff

either
\mathcal{M},\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}i\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}\not\models\varphi
for some
{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}\in\Gamma
or
\mathcal{M},\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.2965pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-3.2965pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 3.2965pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.90543pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-2.90543pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 2.90543pt$}}}j\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.2965pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-3.2965pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 3.2965pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.90543pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-2.90543pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 2.90543pt$}}}\models\psi
for some
{j}\mathord{:}{\psi}\in\Delta
,

in which case we say that
\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta
is true for
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
. Otherwise, it is false.

S
is valid in a class
\mathcal{C}
of models, written
\mathcal{C}\models S
, iff
S
is true for all interpretations
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
of
S
into models
\mathcal{M}\in\mathcal{C}
.

The notion of validity does not change if interpretations are defined exactly on the labels
\mathtt{L}(S)
of a sequent
S
rather than on any superset
X\supseteq\mathtt{L}(S)
thereof.

Remark 59
.

An ordinary sequent
\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta
can be viewed as a single-label labelled sequent
{1}\mathord{:}{\Gamma}\Rightarrow{1}\mathord{:}{\Delta}
with
\mathcal{R}=\varnothing
, where
{i}\mathord{:}{\Pi}\mathrel{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle:$}}}{=}}\{{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}\mid\varphi\in\Pi\}
. This representation is faithful: any mapping
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
defined on
1
is an interpretation of
{1}\mathord{:}{\Gamma}\Rightarrow{1}\mathord{:}{\Delta}
and
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models{1}\mathord{:}{\Gamma}\Rightarrow{1}\mathord{:}{\Delta}
iff
\mathcal{M},\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.22217pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{1}\right.$}\kern-4.22217pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{1}\right.$}}\kern 4.22217pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.22217pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{1}\right.$}\kern-4.22217pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{1}\right.$}}\kern 4.22217pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.40552pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{1}\right.$}\kern-3.40552pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{1}\right.$}}\kern 3.40552pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.97221pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{1}\right.$}\kern-2.97221pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{1}\right.$}}\kern 2.97221pt$}}}1\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.22217pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{1}\right.$}\kern-4.22217pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{1}\right.$}}\kern 4.22217pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.22217pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{1}\right.$}\kern-4.22217pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{1}\right.$}}\kern 4.22217pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.40552pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{1}\right.$}\kern-3.40552pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{1}\right.$}}\kern 3.40552pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.97221pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{1}\right.$}\kern-2.97221pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{1}\right.$}}\kern 2.97221pt$}}}\models\bigwedge\Gamma\rightarrow\bigvee\Delta
.

\lipicsEnd

Inference rules and proofs for ordinary sequents are defined in
\cref
subsec:seqcal, so we will use the same notation. Examples of labelled sequent rules can be found in
\cref
eq:label_sample_rules,eq:label_modal_rules,eq:label_special_rules which will be explained in more detail in the next paragraphs. We will typically omit the word
labelled
, while talking about sequents, rules, etc. to mean the labelled versions and using
unlabelled
or
ordinary
otherwise. Names of labelled calculi are prefixed with
\mathbf{L}
and names of labelled rules are prefixed with
\mathsf{L}
.

\scriptstyle\mathsf{Lid}^{*}\;

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta,{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi},{i}\mathord{:}{\psi},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

\scriptstyle\mathsf{L}\wedge_{l}\;

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi\wedge\psi},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta,{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\psi},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

\scriptstyle\mathsf{L}\rightarrow_{l}\;

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi\rightarrow\psi},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

Labelled versions of some propositional sequent rules from
\cref
fig:G3K.

iRj,\mathcal{R},{j}\mathord{:}{\varphi},{i}\mathord{:}{\Box\varphi},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

\scriptstyle\mathsf{L}\Box_{l}\;

iRj,\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\Box\varphi},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

\scriptstyle\mathsf{L}\Box_{r}\;

\;{(j\notin\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta)}

\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta,{i}\mathord{:}{\Box\varphi}

Labelled sequent rules for the only modality
\Box
of language
\mathcal{L}_{\Box}
.

jRi,iRj,\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

\scriptstyle\mathsf{L}\mathit{B}\;

iRj,\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

iRk,iRj,jRk,\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

\scriptstyle\mathsf{L}{}\mathit{4}\;

iRj,jRk,\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

iRj,\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

\scriptstyle\mathsf{L}{}\mathit{D}\;

\;{(j\notin\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta)}

\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

Figure 21:
Labelled sequent rules for frame conditions of symmetry, transitivity, and seriality.

A labelled sequent calculus typically consists of three groups of rules: propositional rules, modal rules, and special rules. Propositional rules can be obtained by applying the following modification to each of the unlabelled rules from
\cref
fig:G3K (or any other suitable calculus), other than cut.
2
2

2

We omit the treatment of cut, as labelled sequent calculi are typically cut-free.
For an unlabelled rule, add the same relational part
\mathcal{R}
to the conclusion and all premises, treat multisets
\Gamma
,
\Delta
, etc. as multisets of labelled formulas, and assign the same label to all principal and active formulas. The result for three of the rules from
\cref
fig:G3K is shown in
\cref
eq:label_sample_rules. The modal rules require two rules (per modality) and reflect the Kripke semantics for the respective modality. The two rules for language
\mathcal{L}_{\Box}
are shown in
\cref
eq:label_modal_rules.
Special rules are determined on a logic-by-logic basis and can often be generated algorithmically based on the Kripke frame conditions for the logic
[
67
]
. For instance, the rules added if the frames are symmetric, transitive, and serial respectively are shown in
\cref
eq:label_special_rules.

Definition 60
.

Let modal logic
L
be complete w.r.t. a class
\mathcal{C}
of Kripke models. A sequent calculus
\mathbf{LG}
is a
calculus for
L
(w.r.t.
\mathcal{C}
)

iff
\mathbf{LG}\vdash S\Longleftrightarrow\mathcal{C}\models S
for every sequent
S
.

Labelled versions of the propositional rules from
\cref
fig:G3K, i.e., all rules but
(\mathit{K})^{*}
, together with the rules from
\cref
eq:label_modal_rules, form the (cut-free) calculus
\mathbf{L}{\mathbf{G3K}}
(over
\mathcal{L}_{\Box}
) for modal logic
K
. Adding to
\mathbf{L}{\mathbf{G3K}}
any combination of
(\mathsf{L}\mathit{B})
,
(\mathsf{L}\mathit{4})
, and
(\mathsf{L}\mathit{D})
from
\cref
eq:label_special_rules yields calculi for the logics complete w.r.t. the frames of the respective type. Note that some of these logics, e.g,
B
, possess no known cut-free
unlabelled
sequent calculus. Cf.
[
67
]
for general methods of constructing cut-free labelled sequent calculi.

Proposition 61
.

If
\mathbf{LG}
is a calculus for
L
, then
\textnormal{{L}}\vdash\varphi\rightarrow\psi
iff
\mathbf{LG}\vdash{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}\Rightarrow{i}\mathord{:}{\psi}
for any
i
.

5.2
Labelled Interpolation

As in the unlabelled case, an interpolant of a theorem
\varphi\rightarrow\psi
is found based on a proof of a split sequent
{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi};\;\Rightarrow\;;{i}\mathord{:}{\psi}
(for any label
i
). Splits are defined as in
\cref
def:split_seq with relational atoms remaining outside of the split. The left (right) side of the split sequent
\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}
is
\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta
(resp.
\mathcal{R},\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta^{\prime}
). Split rules are obtained as in the unlabelled case. For the modal rules from
\cref
eq:label_modal_rules,
\Gamma
and
\Delta
are split the same way in the premise and conclusion, whereas both
{i}\mathord{:}{\Box\varphi}
and
{j}\mathord{:}{\varphi}
in the premise have to be on the same side of the split as
{i}\mathord{:}{\Box\varphi}
in the conclusion.

Since labelled formulas are interpreted at different Kripke worlds according to a given interpretation
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
, the same should hold for interpolants, which cannot be mere formulas, but rather boolean combinations
3
3

3

Conjunctions and disjunctions are often sufficient with additional connectives added if need be.
of labelled formulas:

Definition 62

(Multiformula)
.

Grammar

\mho\mathrel{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle:$}}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle:$}}}{=}}{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}\mid(\mho\mathbin{\varovee}\mho)\mid(\mho\mathbin{\varowedge}\mho)

where
\varphi\in{\sf Form}_{\mathcal{L}}
and
i\in\mathbb{N}
defines
multiformulas
(over a language
\mathcal{L}
).
i\in\mho
denotes that label
i
occurs in
\mho
.

Definition 63

(Multiformula semantics)
.

Let
\mho
be a multiformula and let
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\colon X\to W
be an interpretation into
\mathcal{M}=(W,R,V)
with
X\supseteq\mathtt{L}(\mho)
. Then
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}
iff
\mathcal{M},\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}i\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}\models\varphi
, while
multiformula conjunction

\mathbin{\varowedge}
and
disjunction

\mathbin{\varovee}
behave classically, i.e.,
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mho_{1}\mathbin{\varowedge}\mho_{2}
(
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mho_{1}\mathbin{\varovee}\mho_{2}
) iff
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mho_{j}
for both
j=1,2
(resp. for at least one of
j=1,2
). We say that two multiformulas
\mho
and
\mho^{\prime}
are
logically equivalent
, iff for any model
\mathcal{M}
and any interpretation
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
of
\mho
and
\mho^{\prime}
into
\mathcal{M}
, we have
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mho
iff
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mho^{\prime}
.

\mathsf{Var}(\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta)
and
\mathsf{Var}(\mho)
are defined by ignoring
\mathcal{R}
, as well as all the labels.

Definition 64

(Labelled Craig interpolation)
.

A calculus
\mathbf{LG}
over a language
\mathcal{L}
has the
Labelled Craig Interpolation Property (LCIP)
w.r.t. class
\mathcal{C}
of Kripke models iff, whenever
\vdash_{\mathbf{LG}}S
for a sequent
S=\mathcal{R},\Gamma,\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta,\Delta^{\prime}
, there exists a multiformula
\mho
over
\mathcal{L}
such that

1.

\mathsf{Var}(\mho)\subseteq\mathsf{Var}(\Gamma,\Delta)\cap\mathsf{Var}(\Gamma^{\prime},\Delta^{\prime})
, i.e., all atoms in
\mho
occur in both sides of the split;

2.

for any interpretation
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
of
S
into a Kripke model
\mathcal{M}\in\mathcal{C}
:

[a]

(a)

if
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\not\models\mho
, then
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta
and

(b)

if
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mho
, then
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R},\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta^{\prime}
;

3.

\mathtt{L}(\mho)\subseteq\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{R},\Gamma,\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta,\Delta^{\prime})
, i.e., all labels in
\mho
occur in the sequent.

In this case, we write
\vdash_{\mathbf{LG}}\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}
and call
\mho
the
(labelled Craig) interpolant
for the split sequent
\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}
in
\mathbf{LG}
.

Here conditions
1
–
2
are labelled analogs of the two conditions from
\cref
def:Maehara, with condition
2a
being contrapositive relative to the first part of
\cref
def:Maehara(
2
). Condition
3
is labelled-specific (also used for nested and hypersequents). For the LIP, as in
\cref
def:sequent_Lyndon:

Definition 65

(Labelled Lyndon interpolation)
.

The
Labelled Lyndon Interpolation Property (LLIP)
is obtained by replacing condition
1
in
\cref
def:msint with:

[1’.]

1.

\mathsf{Var^{+}}(\mho)\subseteq\mathsf{Var^{-}}(\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta)\cap\mathsf{Var^{+}}(\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta^{\prime})
and
\mathsf{Var^{-}}(\mho)\subseteq\mathsf{Var^{+}}(\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta)\cap\mathsf{Var^{-}}(\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta^{\prime})
.

Theorem 66
.

Let
\mathbf{LG}
be a calculus for
L
(both over
\mathcal{L}_{\Box}
) w.r.t. a class
\mathcal{C}
of Kripke models. If
\mathbf{LG}
has the LCIP/LLIP, then
L
has the CIP/LIP.

Proof sketch.

If
\mho
is a labelled Craig/Lyndon interpolant for
{1}\mathord{:}{\varphi};\;\Rightarrow\;;{1}\mathord{:}{\psi}
in
\mathbf{LG}
, then one can prove that formula
\mathop{\mathsf{form}}(\mho)
is a Craig/Lyndon interpolant for
\varphi\rightarrow\psi
in
L
, where
\mathop{\mathsf{form}}(\mho)
is defined by
\mathop{\mathsf{form}}({i}\mathord{:}{\varphi})\mathrel{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle:$}}}{=}}\varphi
,
\mathop{\mathsf{form}}(\mho_{1}\circledcirc\mho_{2})\mathrel{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle:$}}}{=}}\mathop{\mathsf{form}}(\mho_{1})\circ\mathop{\mathsf{form}}(\mho_{2})
for
\circ\in\{\wedge,\vee\}
.
∎

Now we turn to a reverse engineering strategy to see how to define interpolant transformations for various labelled rules. The form of the rules will guide us on how to come up with a construction of appropriate interpolant transformations.

For this new presentation we will divide labelled rules into different categories depending on the shape of their split rules. Different categories will require different interpolant transformations. When going through these explanations, the reader might notice many similarities with the interpolant constructions from
\cref
fig:interpolation for unlabelled rules. Indeed, many of the (implicit) principles in
\cref
fig:interpolation transfer to the labelled case with minor modifications. A fundamental difference is that interpolants in the labelled case are multiformulas (contain labels) instead of just formulas, which means that here we need the multiformula variants
\mathbin{\varowedge}
and
\mathbin{\varovee}
of
\wedge
and
\vee
respectively. The reverse engineering strategy leads to the interpolant transformations for propositional labelled rules presented in
\cref
fig:lab_interpolation, which show close similarities with
\cref
fig:interpolation for unlabelled rules.

Interpolants for the four splits of labelled axiom
(\mathsf{Lid}^{*})

\scriptstyle\mathit{LR}(\mathsf{Lid}^{*})\;

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}

\scriptstyle\mathit{RR}(\mathsf{Lid}^{*})\;

\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}\xRightarrow{{i}\mathord{:}{\top}}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}

\scriptstyle\mathit{LL}(\mathsf{Lid}^{*})\;

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\ \xRightarrow{{i}\mathord{:}{\bot}}{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi},\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\scriptstyle\mathit{RL}(\mathsf{Lid}^{*})\;

\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}\xRightarrow{{i}\mathord{:}{\neg\varphi}}\Delta,{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi};\Delta^{\prime}

Interpolant transformations for the two splits of unary labelled rule
(\mathsf{L}\wedge_{l})

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi},{i}\mathord{:}{\psi},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\scriptstyle L(\mathsf{L}\wedge_{l})\;

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi\wedge\psi},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi},{i}\mathord{:}{\psi}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\scriptstyle R(\mathsf{L}\wedge_{l})\;

\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi\wedge\psi}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

Interpolant transformations for the two splits of binary labelled rule
(\mathsf{L}\rightarrow_{l})

\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho_{1}}{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi},\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\psi},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho_{2}}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\scriptstyle L(\mathsf{L}\rightarrow_{l})\;

\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi\rightarrow\psi},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho_{1}\mathbin{\varovee}\mho_{2}}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho_{1}}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi}

\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\psi}\xRightarrow{\mho_{2}}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\scriptstyle R(\mathsf{L}\rightarrow_{l})\;

\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\varphi\rightarrow\psi}\xRightarrow{\mho_{1}\mathbin{\varowedge}\mho_{2}}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

Figure 22:
Craig/Lyndon interpolant construction for sample split rules obtained from
\cref
fig:G3K. Capital
L
(
R
) denotes that the principal formula is on the left (resp. right) side of the split.

We first discuss three types of interpolant transformations that can be categorized via so-called
local
,
conjunctive
, and
disjunctive
split rules. These categories are defined precisely in
[
56
]
, but are in essence defined by the three conditions mentioned in the following three lemmas. In particular, a
local split rule
is a single-premise rule that preserves the local logical consequence (if the premise is true for an interpretation, the conclusion must be true for the same interpretation), which leads to the interpolant of the conclusion being the same as the interpolant of the premise.
For a conjunctive (resp. disjunctive) binary split rule the interpolant of the conclusion is the conjunction (resp. disjunction) of the interpolants of the premises.

Lemma 67

(Local split rules,
[
56
]
)
.

Let
\mathcal{C}
be a class of Kripke models. Assume the following:

[(i)]

1.

\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{R}^{*},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta;\Delta^{\prime})\subseteq\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{R},\Pi;\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma;\Sigma^{\prime})
;

2.

\mathsf{Var}(\Gamma,\Delta)\subseteq\mathsf{Var}(\Pi,\Sigma)\quad\text{and}\quad\mathsf{Var}(\Gamma^{\prime},\Delta^{\prime})\subseteq\mathsf{Var}(\Pi^{\prime},\Sigma^{\prime})
;

3.

For all
\mathcal{M}\in\mathcal{C}
and interpretations
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
of
\mathcal{R},\Pi;\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma;\Sigma^{\prime}
into
\mathcal{M}
:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R}^{*},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta\quad

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\quad\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R},\Pi\Rightarrow\Sigma,\text{ and }

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R}^{*},\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta^{\prime}\quad

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\quad\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R},\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma^{\prime}.

Then the following interpolant transformation works for the LCIP:

{\vbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{$\mathcal{R}^{*},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}$}}}}\kern 1.43518pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\smash{\lower 2.5pt\hbox{$$}}$}}\vbox{\vbox to0.4pt{\vfill\hbox to65.8889pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt}\vfill}}\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\smash{\lower 2.5pt\hbox{$$}}$}\hss}\kern 0.0pt}\kern 1.43518pt\hbox{\kern 2.15974pt\hbox{\hbox{$\kern 0.0pt\hbox{$\mathcal{R},\Pi;\Pi^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Sigma;\Sigma^{\prime}$}\kern 0.0pt$}}\kern 2.15974pt}}}\kern 0.0pt}}}\ ,

i.e., if
\mho
is a labelled Craig interpolant for
\mathcal{R}^{*},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}
, then
\mho
is also a labelled Craig interpolant for
\mathcal{R},\Pi;\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma;\Sigma^{\prime}
.

Lemma 68

(Conjunctive split rules,
[
56
]
)
.

Let
\mathcal{C}
be a class of Kripke models. Assume:

[(i)]

1.

\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{R}_{a},\Gamma_{a};\Gamma_{a}^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta_{a};\Delta_{a}^{\prime})\subseteq\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{R},\Pi;\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma;\Sigma^{\prime})

for both
a\in\{1,2\}
;

2.

\mathsf{Var}(\Gamma_{a},\Delta_{a})\subseteq\mathsf{Var}(\Pi,\Sigma)\quad\text{and}\quad\mathsf{Var}(\Gamma_{a}^{\prime},\Delta_{a}^{\prime})\subseteq\mathsf{Var}(\Pi^{\prime},\Sigma^{\prime})\ \text{for both $a\in\{1,2\}$}
;

3.

For all
\mathcal{M}\in\mathcal{C}
and interpretations

(Disjunctive split rules,

.

3.

For all

and interpretations
