\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R}_{1},\Gamma^{\prime}_{1}\Rightarrow\Delta^{\prime}_{1}\quad

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\quad\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R},\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma^{\prime},\text{ and }

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R}_{2},\Gamma^{\prime}_{2}\Rightarrow\Delta^{\prime}_{2}\quad

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\quad\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R},\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma^{\prime}.

Then the following interpolant transformation works for the LCIP:

{\small{{}{}\vbox{\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox{\vbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{\hbox{$\mathcal{R}_{1},\Gamma_{1};\Gamma_{1}^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho_{1}}\Delta_{1};\Delta_{1}^{\prime}$}}\kern 9.24994pt}\hbox{\hbox{$\mathcal{R}_{2},\Gamma_{2};\Gamma_{2}^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho_{2}}\Delta_{2};\Delta_{2}^{\prime}$}}}}}\kern 1.29166pt\hbox{\kern 0.0pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\smash{\lower 2.31248pt\hbox{$$}}$}}\vbox{\vbox to0.4pt{\vfill\hbox to161.80417pt{\leaders{\hrule}{\hfill}\kern 0.0pt}\vfill}}\hbox to0.0pt{\hbox{$\smash{\lower 2.31248pt\hbox{$$}}$}\hss}\kern 0.0pt}\kern 1.29166pt\hbox{\kern 41.51483pt\hbox{\hbox{$\mathcal{R},\Pi;\Pi^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho_{1}\mathbin{\varovee}\mho_{2}}\Sigma;\Sigma^{\prime}$}}\kern 41.51483pt}}}\kern 0.0pt}}}}\ .

The transformations in
\cref
lem:local_gen,lem:conjunct_gen,lem:disjunct_gen also work for the LLIP if assumptions (
2
) are strengthened to satisfy
\cref
def:lablyndint(
1^{\prime}
), taking polarity into account (for details see
[
56
]
).

Before we provide examples of labelled local, conjunctive, and disjunctive split rules, we note that these concepts can already be observed in the unlabelled case.

Example 70

(The unlabelled case)
.

Recall from
\cref
rem:seq_as_lab_seq that an ordinary sequent can be viewed as a labelled sequent with a single label. Applying the conditions of
\cref
lem:local_gen,lem:conjunct_gen,lem:disjunct_gen to rules in
\cref
fig:interpolation, we see that
R(\wedge_{l}^{i})
,
R(\rightarrow_{r})
, and
L(\mathsf{c}_{r})
are local split rules,
L(\vee_{l})
,
L(\wedge_{r})
, and
L(\rightarrow_{l})
are disjunctive split rules, while
R(\vee_{l})
,
R(\wedge_{r})
, and
R(\rightarrow_{l})
are conjunctive split rules. Note the symmetry that often occurs for the Left/Right splits of a rule vs. disjunctive/conjunctive behavior of the interpolant construction. A similar pattern can be seen in the interpolant transformation for the analytic cut rule in the proof of
\cref
thm:interpolationSvijf.

For modal unlabelled rules, consider
\cref
fig:modal-rules. Any split of rule
(\mathit{T})
is local w.r.t. any class
\mathcal{C}
of reflexive models: if
\varphi
is false in a reflexive world, then so is
\Box\varphi
. This explains the interpolant preservation for
L(\mathit{T})
in
\cref
fig:modal_Craig_transformations.
By contrast, the other rules in
\cref
fig:modal-rules are not local, hence, require non-trivial transformations in
\cref
fig:modal_Craig_transformations.
\lipicsEnd

Let us now consider the labelled analog of
\cref
ex:unlabelled-modal-rules.

Example 71

(The labelled case)
.

For the propositional rules in
\cref
fig:lab_interpolation, we can identify local, conjunctive, and disjunctive split rules according to essentially the same argument as in
\cref
ex:unlabelled-modal-rules. For modal labelled rules, the argument is different from the unlabelled case. The standard split version of
(\mathsf{L}\Box_{l})
from
\cref
eq:label_modal_rules is local (w.r.t. any class) because
\mathcal{M},\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.2965pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-3.2965pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 3.2965pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.90543pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-2.90543pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 2.90543pt$}}}j\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.2965pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-3.2965pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 3.2965pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.90543pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-2.90543pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 2.90543pt$}}}\not\models\varphi
and
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}i\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}R\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.2965pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-3.2965pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 3.2965pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.90543pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-2.90543pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 2.90543pt$}}}j\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 4.09836pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-4.09836pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 4.09836pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 3.2965pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-3.2965pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 3.2965pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.90543pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}\kern-2.90543pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{j}\right.$}}\kern 2.90543pt$}}}
imply
\mathcal{M},\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}i\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 3.60022pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.94278pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.94278pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.94278pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.67822pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-2.67822pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{i}\right.$}}\kern 2.67822pt$}}}\not\models\Box\varphi
. Consequently, the following interpolant transformations work for the LCIP/LLIP w.r.t. any class of models:

iRj,\mathcal{R},{j}\mathord{:}{\varphi},{i}\mathord{:}{\Box\varphi},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\scriptstyle L(\mathsf{L}\Box_{l})\;

iRj,\mathcal{R},{i}\mathord{:}{\Box\varphi},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

iRj,\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\Box\varphi},{j}\mathord{:}{\varphi}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

\scriptstyle R(\mathsf{L}\Box_{l})\;

iRj,\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime},{i}\mathord{:}{\Box\varphi}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

However, the left and right split versions of
(\mathsf{L}\Box_{r})
cannot be identified as local, conjunctive, or disjunctive and need different treatments as discussed in
\cref
Ex: box diamond.
\lipicsEnd

In addition, many of the unary special labelled rules are local, hence, require no change to the interpolant. A large class of special rules is obtained by so-called Horn clauses. Restricting a class of frames by a Horn clause of the form
i_{1}Rj_{1}\wedge\dots\wedge i_{m}Rj_{m}\rightarrow kRl
corresponds to adding to the labelled calculus the rule

kRl,i_{1}Rj_{1},\dots,i_{m}Rj_{m},\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

\;{(k,l\in i_{1}Rj_{1},\dots,i_{m}Rj_{m},\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta)}

i_{1}Rj_{1},\dots,i_{m}Rj_{m},\mathcal{R},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta

(2)

along with the
rules obtained from it by the
closure condition
, i.e., by contracting identical relational atoms
i_{a}Rj_{a}
and
i_{b}Rj_{b}
from the conclusion in both premise and conclusion for those instances of the rule that contain such identical atoms
[
67
, Th. 11.27, Cor. 11.29]
. Note that in the absence of a principal formula, such rules only have one corresponding split rule (instead of a left and right split version).

Example 72

(Horn frame conditions,
[
55
]
)
.

For any split of (
2
) or its contractions, the following interpolant transformation works for the LCIP/LLIP w.r.t. any class of models satisfying the condition
i_{1}Rj_{1}\wedge\dots\wedge i_{m}Rj_{m}\rightarrow kRl
:

kRl,i_{1}Rj_{1},\dots,i_{m}Rj_{m},\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

i_{1}Rj_{1},\dots,i_{m}Rj_{m},\mathcal{R},\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\xRightarrow{\mho}\Delta;\Delta^{\prime}

Consequently, the split version of rule
(\mathsf{L}\mathit{B})
for any class of symmetric frames and of rule
(\mathsf{L}\mathit{4})
for any class of transitive frames from
\cref
eq:label_special_rules are local and, therefore, the above interpolant transformation works for the LCIP/LLIP.
\lipicsEnd

Two rules from
\cref
eq:label_modal_rules,eq:label_special_rules do not fit any of the patterns described so far: the premise of both
(\mathsf{L}\Box_{r})
and
(\mathsf{L}\mathit{D})
has a fresh label not occurring in the conclusion. Such rules require a more complex transformation to remove the extra label from premise interpolants. For that, we introduce additional notation for modifying interpretations.

Definition 73
.

Let
\mathcal{M}=(W,R,V)
be a Kripke model and
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\colon X\to W
map labels from
X\subseteq\mathbb{N}
to worlds of
\mathcal{M}
. For
v\in W
and
j\in\mathbb{N}
, we define

\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}_{j}^{v}\mathrel{\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\displaystyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle:$}}}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle:$}}}{=}}\bigl(\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\setminus(\{j\}\times W)\bigr)\sqcup\{(j,v)\}

where
\sqcup
is used to emphasize that this is the union of disjoint sets.

The domain of
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}_{j}^{v}
is
X\cup\{j\}
. Note that the only possible difference between
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}_{j}^{v}
and
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
is that the former maps
j
to
v
. Note also that if
j\notin\mathtt{L}(S)
for a labelled sequent
S
, then
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
is an interpretation for
S
iff
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}_{j}^{v}
is and, if both are,
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models S
iff
\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}_{j}^{v}\models S
for any
v\in W
. The same applies to multiformulas.

Definition 74
.

Let
\mathcal{M}=(W,R,V)
be a Kripke model and
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\colon X\to W
be an interpretation of a sequent

. For

and

, we write

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}_{j}^{iR\forall}\models S

\displaystyle\qquad\text{ whenever }\qquad\forall v\in W\text{ such that }\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.60022pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{i}\right.$}\kern-3.60022pt\hbox 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}_{j}^{v}\models S.

Definition 75
.

A label
j
is
\mathbin{\varowedge}\mathbin{\varovee}
-separated
(resp.
\mathbin{\varovee}\mathbin{\varowedge}
-separated
) in a multiformula
\mho
iff for some formulas
B_{1},\dots,B_{m}
and multiformulas
\mho_{1},\dots,\mho_{m}
with
j\notin\mathtt{L}(\mho_{k})
for any
1\leq k\leq m
, we have
\mho=\mathop{{{{{\varowedge}}}}}\limits_{k=1}^{m}({j}\mathord{:}{B_{k}}\mathbin{\varovee}\mho_{k})
(resp.
\mho=\mathop{{{{{\varovee}}}}}\limits_{k=1}^{m}({j}\mathord{:}{B_{k}}\mathbin{\varowedge}\mho_{k})
).

Proposition 76
.

Let
j\in\mathbb{N}
. For any multiformula
\mho
, there is a logically equivalent multiformula
\mho^{\prime}
such that
j
is
\mathbin{\varowedge}\mathbin{\varovee}
-separated (resp.
\mathbin{\varovee}\mathbin{\varowedge}
-separated) in
\mho^{\prime}
.

Proof.

\mho^{\prime}
can be constructed by rewritings similar to the ones used for CNF (resp. DNF), in view of logical equivalences of
{j}\mathord{:}{\varphi}\mathbin{\varowedge}{j}\mathord{:}{\psi}
to
{j}\mathord{:}{\varphi\wedge\psi}
and of
{j}\mathord{:}{\varphi}\mathbin{\varovee}{j}\mathord{:}{\psi}
to
{j}\mathord{:}{\varphi\vee\psi}
. If
{j}\mathord{:}{B_{k}}
is absent for some
k
, either
{j}\mathord{:}{\top}\mathbin{\varowedge}
or
{j}\mathord{:}{\bot}\mathbin{\varovee}
is added, whichever is logically equivalent.
∎

\Cref

prop:label_norm_form does not guarantee the uniqueness of
\mho^{\prime}
, but given
\mho
we know there is at least one logically equivalent multiformula
\mho^{\prime}
with
j

\mathbin{\varowedge}\mathbin{\varovee}
-separated (resp.
\mathbin{\varovee}\mathbin{\varowedge}
-separated) in it. From now on, given a multiformula
\mho
, we allow ourselves to pick one such
\mho^{\prime}
and denote it by
\mho_{\mathbin{\varowedge}j\mathbin{\varovee}}
(resp.
\mho_{\mathbin{\varovee}j\mathbin{\varowedge}}
). For a multiformula
\mho
, we further write
\mho^{j\mapsto i\Box}
(resp.
\mho^{j\mapsto i\Diamond}
) to mean the result of replacing each occurrence of
{j}\mathord{:}{}
in
\mho
with
{i}\mathord{:}{\Box}
(resp. with
{i}\mathord{:}{\Diamond}
).

Example 77
.

Suppose
\mho_{\mathbin{\varowedge}j\mathbin{\varovee}}=\mathop{{{{{\varowedge}}}}}\limits_{k=1}^{m}({j}\mathord{:}{B_{k}}\mathbin{\varovee}\mho_{k})
(resp.
\mho_{\mathbin{\varovee}j\mathbin{\varowedge}}=\mathop{{{{{\varovee}}}}}\limits_{k=1}^{m}({j}\mathord{:}{B_{k}}\mathbin{\varowedge}\mho_{k})
) with
j\notin\mathtt{L}(\mho_{k})
for any
1\leq k\leq m
. Then,

\displaystyle\mho_{\mathbin{\varowedge}j\mathbin{\varovee}}^{j\mapsto i\Box}=\mathop{{{{{\varowedge}}}}}\limits_{k=1}^{m}({i}\mathord{:}{\Box B_{k}}\mathbin{\varovee}\mho_{k})\qquad\big(\text{respectively}\qquad\mho_{\mathbin{\varovee}j\mathbin{\varowedge}}^{j\mapsto i\Diamond}=\mathop{{{{{\varovee}}}}}\limits_{k=1}^{m}({i}\mathord{:}{\Diamond B_{k}}\mathbin{\varowedge}\mho_{k})\big).

Clearly,
j\notin\mathtt{L}(\mho_{\mathbin{\varowedge}j\mathbin{\varovee}}^{j\mapsto i\Box})
(resp.
j\notin\mathtt{L}(\mho_{\mathbin{\varovee}j\mathbin{\varowedge}}^{j\mapsto i\Diamond})
).
\lipicsEnd

The general transformation applicable to rules
(\mathsf{L}\Box_{r})
and
(\mathsf{L}\mathit{D})
additionally exploits the fact that the fresh label
j
is only connected to one existing label
i
by one relational atom
iRj
.

Lemma 78

(
\Box
-like rules,
[
56
]
)
.

Let
\mathcal{C}
be a class of Kripke models. Assume:

[(i)]

1.

\mathsf{Var}(\Gamma,\Delta)\subseteq\mathsf{Var}(\Pi,\Sigma)\quad\text{and}\quad\mathsf{Var}(\Gamma^{\prime},\Delta^{\prime})\subseteq\mathsf{Var}(\Pi^{\prime},\Sigma^{\prime})
,

2.

j\notin\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{R},\Pi;\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma;\Sigma^{\prime})\quad\text{and}\quad\{i\}\cup\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{R}^{*})\subseteq\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{R},\Pi;\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma;\Sigma^{\prime})
,

3.

\mathtt{L}(\Gamma;\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta;\Delta^{\prime})\subseteq\{j\}\sqcup\mathtt{L}(\mathcal{R},\Pi;\Pi^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Sigma;\Sigma^{\prime})
,

4.

For all
\mathcal{M}\in\mathcal{C}
and interpretations
\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}
of

into

:

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}^{iR\exists}_{j}\models iRj,\mathcal{R}^{*},\Gamma\Rightarrow\Delta\quad

\displaystyle\Longrightarrow\quad\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\models\mathcal{R},\Pi\Rightarrow\Sigma,\ \text{and}

\displaystyle\mathcal{M}\mathopen{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left[\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}\cdot\mathclose{\mathchoice{\hbox{$\displaystyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\displaystyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\textstyle\kern 3.33328pt\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-3.33328pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\textstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 3.33328pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptstyle\kern 2.76108pt\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.76108pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.76108pt$}}{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\kern 2.52776pt\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}\kern-2.52776pt\hbox to0.0pt{\hss\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle\left]\vphantom{\cdot}\right.$}}\kern 2.52776pt$}}}^{iR\forall}_{j}\models iRj,\mathcal{R}^{*},\Gamma^{\prime}\Rightarrow\Delta^{\prime}\quad

Then the following interpolant transformation works for the LCIP:
